[];\\r\\n  const metadata = await checked<PassageMetadata[]>(rcDb(\\"reading_center_passage_metadata\\").select(\\"*\\").eq(\\"user_id\\", userId));\\r\\n  const byId = new Map((metadata ?? []).map((row) => [row.passage_id, row]));\\r\\n  return (rows ?? []).map((row) => mergePassageMetadata(row, byId.get(row.id))).filter((row): row is Passage => !!row);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function usePassages(raw = false) {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \\"passages\\", raw), queryFn: () => passagesForUser(requireUser(user?.id), raw), enabled: !!user });\\r\\n}\\r\\nexport function usePassageBySlug(slug: string | undefined) {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  return useQuery({\\r\\n    queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \\"passage\\", slug), enabled: !!user && !!slug,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const row = await checked<Passage | null>(rcDb(\\"reading_center_passages\\").select(\\"*\\").eq(\\"slug\\", slug).maybeSingle());\\r\\n      if (!row) return null;\\r\\n      const metadata = await checked<PassageMetadata | null>(rcDb(\\"reading_center_passage_metadata\\").select(\\"*\\").eq(\\"user_id\\", requireUser(user?.id)).eq(\\"passage_id\\", row.id).maybeSingle());\\r\\n      return mergePassageMetadata(row, metadata ?? undefined);\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\nexport function usePreferences() {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \\"prefs\\"), enabled: !!user, queryFn: async (): Promise<Preferences> => {\\r\\n    const row = await checked<Partial<Preferences> | null>(rcDb(\\"reading_center_preferences\\").select(\\"*\\").eq(\\"user_id\\", requireUser(user?.id)).maybeSingle());\\r\\n    return { ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, ...row, line_spacing: Number(row?.line_spacing ?? DEFAULT_PREFERENCES.line_spacing) };\\r\\n  } });\\r\\n}\\r\\nexport function useSavePreferences() {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const client = useQueryClient();\\r\\n  return useMutation({ scope: { id: `rc-preferences-${user?.id}` }, mutationFn: async (input: Partial<Preferences>) => {\\r\\n    const userId = requireUser(user?.id);\\r\\n    // Only changed columns are written so rapid independent controls cannot overwrite one another.\\r\\n    await checked(rcDb(\\"reading_center_preferences\\").upsert({ user_id: userId, ...input, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, { onConflict: \\"user_id\\" }).select(\\"user_id\\").single());\\r\\n    return input;\\r\\n  }, onMutate: async (input) => {\\r\\n    const key = readingCenterKey(user?.id, \\"prefs\\");\\r\\n    await client.cancelQueries({ queryKey: key });\\r\\n    const previous = client.getQueryData<Preferences>(key);\\r\\n    client.setQueryData(key, { ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, ...previous, ...input });\\r\\n    return { previous };\\r\\n  }, onError: (error, _input, context) => {\\r\\n    if (context?.previous) client.setQueryData(readingCenterKey(user?.id, \\"prefs\\"), context.previous);\\r\\n    report(error);\\r\\n  }, onSettled: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \\"prefs\\") }) });\\r\\n}\\r\\nexport function useProgressList() {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \\"progress\\"), enabled: !!user, queryFn: () => checked<Progress[]>(rcDb(\\"reading_center_progress\\").select(\\"*\\").eq(\\"user_id\\", requireUser(user?.id))) });\\r\\n}\\r\\nexport interface SaveProgressInput { passage_id: string; word_index: number; completed?: boolean; hidden_from_recent?: boolean }\\r\\nexport function useSaveProgress() {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const client = useQueryClient();\\r\\n  return useMutation({ scope: { id: `rc-progress-${user?.id}` }, mutationFn: async (input: SaveProgressInput) => {\\r\\n    const userId = requireUser(user?.id);\\r\\n    // Only update explicit fields: opening at zero preserves completion; restart explicitly clears it.\\r\\n    await checked(rcDb(\\"reading_center_progress\\").upsert(progressPayload(userId, input), { onConflict: \\"user_id,passage_id\\" }).select(\\"passage_id,word_index,completed_at\\").single());\\r\\n    if (input.word_index > 0 || input.completed) {\\r\\n      // A finished passage is part of ongoing reading practice, not the whole assignment.\\r\\n      await checked(supabase.from(\\"classroom_items\\").update({ status: \\"in_progress\\" })\\r\\n        .eq(\\"student_id\\", userId).eq(\\"source_table\\", \\"reading_center\\").eq(\\"source_id\\", \\"library\\")\\r\\n        .eq(\\"visible_to_student\\", true).eq(\\"status\\", \\"assigned\\").select(\\"id\\"));\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }, onError: report, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \\"progress\\") }) });\\r\\n}\\r\\nexport function useBookmarks(passageId?: string) {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \\"bookmarks\\", passageId), enabled: !!user && !!passageId, queryFn: () => checked<Bookmark[]>(rcDb(\\"reading_center_bookmarks\\").select(\\"*\\").eq(\\"user_id\\", requireUser(user?.id)).eq(\\"passage_id\\", passageId).order(\\"created_at\\", { ascending: false })) });\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction useScopedMutation<T, R>(mutationFn: (input: T, userId: string) => Promise<R>, scope?: string) {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const client = useQueryClient();\\r\\n  return useMutation({ mutationFn: (input: T) => mutationFn(input, requireUser(user?.id)), onError: report, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, ...(scope ? ## assistant

One high-priority defect remains: **eReader reopening resets saved progress to page 1.**

`ReaderPage.tsx` computes the saved page before `EReader.tsx` finishes measuring its columns. Once measurement completes, page 0 invokes `onPageWord`, overwriting the saved position. An actual-component React harness reproduced saved word 180 becoming word 0/page 0.

Restore the saved word after column layout is ready, and suppress position writes until restoration finishes.

Earlier blockers are resolved: completion awaits successful persistence with retry UI; Tags uses a valid column; bookmark writes verify affected rows. No additional high-priority authorization, CSS leakage, dependency, or worker-path defect found.

## assistant

eReader resume passes the final source review.

One critical autosave race remains: typing while a new bookmark’s first save is pending can lose the newer text when the dialog switches from creation to editing and reloads the older saved note. A slow save can also allow a second creation request.

Preserve dirty text through that transition and serialize saves, or disable editing while saving with a save-in-flight guard. The existing bookmark update/retry flow otherwise passes.

After resolution: the full answer and explanation.\\n- Questions, explanations, ordering and earned dials stay exactly as they are.\\n\\n## Content\\n- Add an `answer_key` to each of the 48 items in `spot-the-relationship.json`, kept separate from `explanation`:\\n  - `relationships[]`: `type` (conditional | causal | comparative), `indicators[]` (accepted spans, with separated phrases like \\"more … than\\" stored as required parts), `connects` (what the words link), `why` (why that type).\\n  - `traps[]`: incomplete or tempting wrong answers (e.g. \\"requires\\" labelled causal, \\"deep\\" as a comparison).\\n  - `context_benchmark` where a comparison relies on an earlier state or number (items 31, 36, 47, and any others).\\n- Keys are drafted from the existing credited answers and explanations, then checked item by item; no question text or explanation is rewritten.\\n- Add the grading rubric (Correct / Partial / Incorrect definitions, equivalent names, capitalisation, quote style, span tolerance, both halves of split comparatives, shared types, contextual comparisons) to the activity\'s coach instructions.\\n\\n## Instant grading\\n- New local pair checker: parses the answer into type/word pairs, normalises type synonyms, case and quotes, and returns `correct` only when every keyed type has a valid indicator with nothing extra or contradictory. Anything uncertain returns \\"send to AI\\" rather than guessing wrong.\\n\\n## AI grading (server side)\\n- The server already loads the trusted item from the assignment; extend that payload to include the `answer_key` alongside the sentence, explanation, rubric and attempt number. The student answer stays marked as untrusted data.\\n- Update the coach prompt to judge pairs and to hint without revealing on attempt 1.\\n\\n## Assignments\\n- Completed and in-progress assignment snapshots are left untouched.\\n- Unstarted assignments of this activity get the new content; the library copy is updated too. Read back afterwards to confirm.\\n\\n## Tests\\n- All 48 correct answers pass instantly; type-only, words-only, missing type, invented type, swapped and contradictory pairs route correctly; contextual comparisons; first-attempt hint and final explanation display; a captured server-to-model payload (mocked model, no paid calls).\\n\\n## Technical details\\n- Files: `content/spot-the-relationship.json`, a new `relationshipKey.ts` checker + tests, `ActivityItemView.tsx` (explanation on correct, routing), `supabase/functions/activity-coach/grading.ts` (prompt + key), a forward-only migration replacing the payload builder in `begin_activity_ai_request_v1` to include `answer_key` (receipts compare canonical payload, so it stays consistent), and a data update for library + unstarted assignments.\\n- Deploy only `activity-coach`. Notes use their own gate. */\\r\\nexport default function MyWorkGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const generalAccess = permissions.is_admin || permissions.has_classroom_access;\\r\\n  const readingCenter = useReadingCenterAccess(!generalAccess);\\r\\n  if (!authReady || permissions.loading || (!!user && !generalAccess && readingCenter.isPending)) {\\r\\n    return <div role=\\"status\\" className=\\"student-destination-message\\">Checking assigned work access…</div>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return <ProtectedRoute allowSignedIn>\\r\\n    {generalAccess || readingCenter.allowed ? children : readingCenter.isError\\r\\n      ? <div role=\\"alert\\" className=\\"student-destination-message\\">We could not verify access to assigned work. <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"classroom-button\\" onClick={() => void readingCenter.refetch()}>Try again</button></div>\\r\\n      : <LockedModule />}\\r\\n  </ProtectedRoute>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

A student who finished the underlying\\r\\n      // assignment must not see it come back because the classroom_items row it\\r\\n      // was mirrored into still says \\"assigned\\" — that is why finished work kept\\r\\n      // reappearing on Home.\\r\\n      const itemStatus = normalizeStatus(item.status);\\r\\n      // Guided and written teacher assignments have their own persisted\\r\\n      // completion/progress evidence. A stale or manually updated mirror must\\r\\n      // not promote them merely because the page was opened or a button was\\r\\n      // pressed outside the actual work flow.\\r\\n      const sourceOwnsStatus = existing && (\\r\\n        sourceTable === \'guided_drill_assignments\' ||\\r\\n        sourceTable === \'homework_assignments\' ||\\r\\n        sourceTable === \'ta_assignments\' ||\\r\\n        sourceTable === \'activity_assignments\'\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      const status = kind === \'plan\' && planStatus && planStatus !== \'skipped\'\\r\\n        ? planStatus\\r\\n        : sourceOwnsStatus\\r\\n        ? existing.status\\r\\n        : itemStatus === \'completed\' || existing?.status === \'completed\' ? \'completed\' : itemStatus;\\r\\n      byIdentity.set(identity, {\\r\\n        key: `ci-${item.id}`,\\r\\n        kind,\\r\\n        refId,\\r\\n        sourceTable,\\r\\n        title: item.title || existing?.title || \'Assigned work\',\\r\\n        // The item carries no question count, so keep the source row\'s subtitle\\r\\n        // when there is one.\\r\\n        subtitle: existing?.subtitle ?? item.description ?? \'\',\\r\\n        status,\\r\\n        createdAt: item.created_at,\\r\\n        dueAt: item.due_at,\\r\\n        // The mirror row was written before the section rule existed, so the\\r\\n        // source row\'s directions route wins over the item\'s classroom link.\\r\\n        route: existing?.selfCompleting ? existing.route : route,\\r\\n        selfCompleting:\\r\\n          (item.metadata_json as { kind?: string } | null)?.kind === \'timed_section\' ||\\r\\n          !!existing?.selfCompleting,\\r\\n        briefing: readSectionBriefing(item.metadata_json) ?? undefined,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n"}}'}]

returns null when absent; throws when present but malformed. */\\n+export function readGradingRubric(raw: unknown): string[] | null {\\n+  if (raw === undefined || raw === null) return null;\\n+  if (!Array.isArray(raw) || !raw.length || raw.length > 50 || !raw.every((r) => text(r, 4000))) {\\n+    throw new Error(\\"Malformed grading rubric\\");\\n+  }\\n+  return raw as string[];\\n+}\\n+\\n+const TYPE_WORDS: Record<RelationshipType, RegExp> = {\\n+  conditional: /\\\\bcondition(?:al|s)?\\\\b/i,\\n+  causal: /\\\\bcaus(?:al|e|es|ation)\\\\b/i,\\n+  comparative: /\\\\bcompar(?:ative|ison|isons|e)\\\\b/i,\\n+};\\n+const norm = (s: string) => s.toLowerCase().replace(/[‘’“”\\"\'`*]/g, \\" \\").replace(/[-_/]/g, \\" \\").replace(/\\\\s+/g, \\" \\");\\n+const has = (hay: string, needle: string) => new RegExp(`\\\\\\\\b${needle.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\\\]\\\\\\\\]/g, \\"\\\\\\\\$&\\")}\\\\\\\\b`).test(hay);\\n+\\n+// Everyday words (\\"so\\", \\"if\\") leak only when the hint singles them out in quotes.\\n+const COMMON = new Set([\\"so\\", \\"if\\", \\"when\\", \\"since\\", \\"as\\", \\"than\\", \\"because\\", \\"unless\\", \\"whenever\\"]);\\n+const quoted = (raw: string, p: string) => new RegExp(`[\\"\'‘“*]\\\\\\\\s*${p}\\\\\\\\s*[\\"\'’”*]`, \\"i\\").test(raw);\\n+\\n+/**\\n+ * First-attempt hint boundary.
